Stock Card Investors and supporters,\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cspan\u003eHappy 4th of July to those of you in the U.S., and greetings to all of you from the rest of the world!\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cspan\u003eToday marks a new chapter in our product's history. In\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://republic.com/stock-card-hoda-mehr-you-are-invited-office-hour-to-disrupt-stock-card-hello\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003e\u003cspan\u003emy April 23rd update\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003cspan\u003e, I mentioned how we are self-disrupting to succeed in the long run. I even spoke with quite a few of you who joined me in the\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://calendly.com/stockcard/disrupt-stock-card-office-hour\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003e\u003cspan\u003e10-day office hours\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003cspan\u003e\u0026nbsp;I held in May 2024. Today, I'm sharing the results of those office hours and our effort to build a better product and company.\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cspan\u003eToday, we've shared a custom GPT,\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://bit.ly/gptCharlie\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003e\u003cspan\u003eCharlie\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003cspan\u003e, with our users, which will drastically change the experience of Stock Card. This AI-powered custom GPT, which we've been training for a while, replaces the old Stock Card visualizations. We plan to integrate Charlie into the website in the coming months while continuing to train it to perform better. In the meantime, you can start testing it by visiting OpenAI's website:\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://bit.ly/gptCharlie\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003e\u003cspan\u003eClick\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan\u003eHere\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cspan\u003eAdvantages of this decision:\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cul\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u003cspan\u003eThis move drastically reduces the costs of running Stock Card because we do not rely on traditional data API purchases from expensive data sources.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u003cspan\u003eIf this model works, we can be profitable even with few subscribers.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u003cspan\u003eThis\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan\u003e\u0026nbsp;could ensure the company's future success. Our user and revenue growth has been slower than what I had wanted to see, especially after Covid's stock market boom. Staying on the old model wasn't a good trajectory for the future.\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\u003c/ul\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cspan\u003eRisks of this decision:\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cul\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u003cspan\u003eIt takes us time to build the new product, integrate it into the website, and learn from our users what they like and don't like. For example, we heard users asking for a stock screener powered by GPT just today. We are excited to learn what else our users ask for.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u003cspan\u003eWe do not own the underlying model behind GPTs. It is owned by OpenAI. Changes to the OpenAI model and features will inevitably impact us.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u003cspan\u003eLike all AI models, custom GPTs make mistakes, hallucinate, and make inaccurate assumptions. While we have spent significant time reducing these issues by setting boundings and definition rules for Charlie, our custom GPT, we cannot guarantee accuracy. It still makes mistakes. We can only inform our users of such issues but cannot fully eliminate them.\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u003cspan\u003eSome VIP users may not like the new direction and tool and\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan\u003eend up canceling\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan\u003e\u0026nbsp;their subscriptions. For example, those who used Stock Card screeners will be disappointed because we still need an AI-enabled screener.\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u003cspan\u003eAnd at the end of our efforts, the GPT-enabled model may fail to attract new users.\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\u003c/ul\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cspan\u003eI acknowledge that this is a risky decision. But we have to be bold! As they say, growth sits at the edges of discomfort and risk-taking.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan\u003eThis\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan\u003e\u0026nbsp;is our way of growing the company. We have to innovate to succeed; otherwise, we won't be able to stay relevant in this rapidly changing market.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cspan\u003eI will keep you informed on our progress, including the possible launch of a new business unit focused on creators and B2B partnerships.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThank you and glad to have you with us!\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eHoda Mehr - Stock Card's Founder\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cem\u003e\u0026nbsp;P.S. Below is a letter I sent to Stock Card's VIP users, which I'd like to share with you, too!\u0026nbsp;\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003chr\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSpecial Letter to Stock Card VIPs - Sent on July 4th, 2024\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eStock Card is self-disrupting\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eI'm writing this letter to our VIP Users to share a bit more about why we made this change and what it means to you. Your feedback over the years has been instrumental in this transition, and I want to ensure that you understand the reasons behind it.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eFor the last twenty years, I have invested in, built, and grown companies and studied some of the best and most well-run companies in history. The most important lesson I've learned is that those who can adopt and self-disrupt have a much higher likelihood of long-term success. That's why Stock Card is making this change.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWhy we are doing this\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eSeven years ago, when I started Stock Card, buying financial data from various sources, aggregating and translating it into easy-to-understand investment research made sense. But, with the rise of generative AI, data aggregation and interpretation can be done for free and are much easier to do than the old way of building a data infrastructure.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThanks to generative AI platforms such as OpenAI, what Stock Card used to do after spending thousands of dollars each month on data acquisition and processing is now available for free. It's important to mention that generative AI isn't perfect, and Charlie still needs a lot of training and testing. But it's time to put the old ways of delivering value aside and embrace the new ways of researching our investment ideas.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eImportant to consider\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWhile we used Stock Card's methodology to train Charlie, the custom GPT, we do not have any control over how the underlying model operates. The underlying model belongs to and is run by OpenAI. Like all generative AI models, Charlie is prone to mistakes, hallucinations, and inaccurate information. As always, you should not make investment decisions solely based on Charlie's output.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eHow to use Stock Card now\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cul\u003e\u003cli\u003eWhile we work on integrating Charlie into the website, you can start testing it on OpenAI's website: \u003ca href=\"https://bit.ly/gptCharlie\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eClick Here\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eOn Stock Card, you can still access the \"Your Portfolio\" feature to track your ideas and compare your investment return with the S\u0026amp;P 500 by visiting this page: \u003ca href=\"https://stockcard.io/myportfolio\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eClick Here\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eYou can join Stock Card's stock-picking contests and not only get new investment ideas but have a chance to earn a $1,000 prize: \u003ca href=\"https://stockcard.io/portfoliostore\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eClick Here\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eVIP users can continue to access premium portfolios and receive notifications for new stock picks the same as before: \u003ca href=\"https://stockcard.io/portfoliostore\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eClick Here\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNEW\u003c/strong\u003e Stock Card VIP users will soon get an invitation to my new Investment Memo - a regular detailed analysis of stocks I buy or hold in my real-money portfolio, exclusive to VIP users: \u003ca href=\"https://hodamehr.substack.com/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eClick Here\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\u003c/ul\u003e\u003cp\u003eI hope you stay a VIP user and participate in creating our new generation of AI-powered platforms.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eAs always, you can manage your subscription on your \u003ca href=\"https://stockcard.io/auth/account/sub\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eaccount management page\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eIf you want to speak with me, email me at \u003ca rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ehoda@stockcard.io\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThank you!\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eHoda -- Stock Card dramatically changed since we launched Stock Card in 2018.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThere is chatGPT to help you with research. New custom GPTs are emerging daily to simplify your research. More investing videos, newsletters, and podcasts go live hourly than we have time to watch, read, and listen to.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWith that context, my team and I at Stock Card have asked ourselves and our current and past paying users what's next for our platform.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eAs they say, in the startup world, \u003cstrong\u003eyou either disrupt yourself or get disrupted by competitors\u003c/strong\u003e. At Stock Card, we plan to self-distrupt! Isn't that exciting!\u003c/p\u003e\u003ch3\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWhat's your take on it?\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/h3\u003e\u003cp\u003eI scheduled a series of \u003ca href=\"https://calendly.com/stockcard/disrupt-stock-card-office-hour\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eVIP user-only office hours\u003c/a\u003e in May:\u003c/p\u003e\u003cul\u003e\u003cli\u003eThese office hours are \u003cstrong\u003eyour chance to participate in Stock Card's self-disruption\u003c/strong\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eThere are \u003cstrong\u003eseveral office hours\u003c/strong\u003e; join \u003ca href=\"https://calendly.com/stockcard/disrupt-stock-card-office-hour\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eany of them\u003c/a\u003e, stay for a while, and share your ideas.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eYou'll help my team and me \u003cstrong\u003eshape the future\u003c/strong\u003e of the tool you pay or have paid to research stocks better and with less noise.\u003c/li\u003e\u003c/ul\u003e\u003cp\u003ePlease\u003ca href=\"https://calendly.com/stockcard/disrupt-stock-card-office-hour\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003e\u0026nbsp;join during office hours\u003c/a\u003e to share your ideas! class=\"fr-inner\"\u003eStock Card content is featured by LinkedIn News Editors\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eHello investors,\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eIn my Q2 update, I told you about our content strategy in 2023. Through several experimentations, we have learned content produced by our team is one of the highest converting sources of user acquisition and retention for Stock Card. That's why we set out to produce high-value content and distribute it through Stock Card's YouTube, Podcast show, and blog.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eContent marketing establishes trust and improves our brand awareness, but it takes time. I'm happy to report that we are gradually seeing the early results of our content strategy. A few achievements:\u003c/p\u003e\u003col\u003e\u003cli\u003eFinancial media are picking up our content and redistributing it. Example: LinkedIn featuring our \u003ca href=\"https://www.linkedin.com/news/story/china-developer-averts-default-5458729/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003epiece on China's Real Estate Crisis\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eThe average number of stocks researched by paying users each month, a key indicator of user engagement and retention, is up by 41% in the last three months, compared to the previous 4-month period. See the chart below.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003ePaying user conversion is at 2.8% -- an excellent conversion rate vs. the industry average.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/li\u003e\u003c/ol\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://uploads.republic.com/p/images/attachments/original/000/096/472/96472-1693949714-fa92d152b5770f14ea7fcdfc9429adec9b3a03e6.png\" class=\"fr-fic fr-dib\"\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eQ4 Priorities:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cul\u003e\u003cli\u003eDespite our achievements, overall market engagement is relatively low, and it is much harder to acquire new users. Our priority is experimenting and finding the most effective organic user acquisition channels. Search Engine Optimization (SEO) is one of the most effective ways to acquire users, and we have developed a comprehensive roadmap of enhancements and improvements for Q4.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eOne of the most unique attributes of our product is how successful it is at engaging users. This means that even if the users are not converting to paying users, they still return to Stock Card to conduct their research and benefit from our free plan. We plan to monetize that free user engagement through referrals to brokerage and selective sponsorship across Stock Card. Exciting features are in the works, and I will report on our referral and advertising revenue progress in my next update.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/li\u003e\u003c/ul\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eIndustry News:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eProminent fintech and WealthTech players have been quite active in the market, taking advantage of the stock market slowdown to acquire good products and teams. The most exciting and latest news is the \u003ca href=\"https://www.axios.com/2023/08/23/commonstock-yahoo-social-investing-platform\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eacquisition of CommonStock by Yahoo! Finance\u003c/a\u003e. Congrats to our good friends at CommonStock!\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNext Update:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eMy next update will be sometime in November, and I hope to share our progress with you then.
